# 7398 body points of squared norm 8: 480 roots, 3840 odd octad vectors on the 30 affine octads, 6 poles and 3072 shoulders.
# 960 added points of squared norm 56/3, scaled here by 3 (squared norm 168): head entries +-3, tail 3*sqrt(8/3) u_theta.
# All body points are scaled by 2 (squared norm 32) so that every coordinate lies in Z[sqrt2, sqrt6].
# Format. Lines starting with # are comments. A number is written as m, m*sqrt(2), m*sqrt(6), sqrt(2), -sqrt(6), etc.
# Each line 'extra x_1,...,x_18' is a nonzero vector p; the configuration is the set of unit vectors p/|p|.
# Any two distinct points x, y satisfy x.y <= |x||y|/2.
